Over an estimated duration of 2 years, 8 to 24 patients with recurrent/refractory NMIBC will be enrolled. The primary objectives are to assess the safety profile, identify the maximum tolerated dose (MTD), and determine the recommended Phase II dose (RP2D) of intravesical VG161.
